Maybe there’s a <a href="https://www.callmepmc.com/grannys-classic-southern-pecan-pie/">pie</a> or two or a <a href="https://www.callmepmc.com/pound-cake-recipes-reviewed/">pound cake</a>, but there must be a three-layer frosted cake at center stage!</p><p>And, I just developed the cake for this year!</p><p>You guys, this Best Toffee Banana Layer Cake is INSANELY delicious!! If you’re looking for an alternative dessert instead of <a href="https://www.callmepmc.com/goo-free-pecan-pie/">pecan pie</a> or pumpkin pie to make for the holidays this year, let me offer this recipe as a suggestion.</p><p>This cake has incredible flavor. Bananas make it super moist and the brown butter frosting is rich and luscious.
